     1	package journal
     2	
     3	// resolution.go is the write side of IN_DOUBT resolution (harden-execution-base
     4	// task 2.7). It is a new file rather than an edit to durability.go: the primitives
     5	// there are released and their tests pin them, and everything below is additive.
     6	//
     7	// Two things exist here that Settle deliberately cannot do:
     8	//
     9	//   - confirming an attempt *and* recording the broker order id the resolution
    10	//     discovered, in one transaction. Settle takes no order id, and a CONFIRMED
    11	//     attempt whose order number is only in a log line is not trackable.
    12	//   - moving out of UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT. Settle refuses that state on purpose
    13	//     (see lifecycle.go); the only way out is an operator saying what happened.
    14	
    15	import (
    16		"context"
    17		"fmt"
    18		"strings"
    19	)
    20	
    21	// Reason codes written by the resolution procedure. Stable strings: alerting and
    22	// the Phase 2 ledger read them.
    23	const (
    24		// ReasonResolvedFound: the mutation was found at the broker.
    25		ReasonResolvedFound = "in_doubt_resolved_found"
    26		// ReasonResolvedAbsent: the mutation was proven absent — stable repeated
    27		// observation plus an account cross-check, never a single empty list.
    28		ReasonResolvedAbsent = "in_doubt_resolved_absent"
    29		// ReasonResolutionExhausted: neither presence nor absence could be proven.
    30		// The attempt is parked for an operator and its symbol stays blocked.
    31		ReasonResolutionExhausted = "in_doubt_unresolved"
    32		// ReasonOperatorResolved: a human closed the attempt.
    33		ReasonOperatorResolved = "operator_resolved"
    34	)
    35	
    36	// ResolveConfirmed closes an IN_DOUBT (or stuck ACKED) attempt as CONFIRMED,
    37	// recording the broker order id the resolution found.
    38	//
    39	// The order id is mandatory: "it exists but we do not know its number" is not a
    40	// resolution, because nothing can later cancel or reconcile it.
    41	//
    42	// It is stored exactly as the broker sent it. `orderId` is an opaque token —
    43	// openapi contracts no shape for it — so trimming, case-folding or any other
    44	// normalisation on the way in would store an identifier the broker never issued,
    45	// and a later byte comparison against the real one would silently fail to match.
    46	// Trimming survives only in the emptiness check, where it asks "is there a name
    47	// here at all".
    48	func (a *Attempt) ResolveConfirmed(ctx context.Context, brokerOrderID, reasonCode, detail string) error {
    49		if strings.TrimSpace(brokerOrderID) == "" {
    50			return fmt.Errorf("%w: confirming an attempt requires the broker order id", ErrInvalidRequest)
    51		}
    52		return a.transition(ctx, StateConfirmed, transitionOpts{
    53			from:          []AttemptState{StateInDoubt, StateAcked},
    54			brokerOrderID: brokerOrderID,
    55			reasonCode:    firstNonEmpty(reasonCode, ReasonResolvedFound),
    56			detail:        detail,
    57			setSettled:    true,
    58		})
    59	}
    60	
    61	// ResolveFailed closes an IN_DOUBT attempt as FAILED_CONFIRMED: the mutation was
    62	// proven not to have happened.
    63	func (a *Attempt) ResolveFailed(ctx context.Context, reasonCode, detail string) error {
    64		return a.transition(ctx, StateFailedConfirmed, transitionOpts{
    65			from:       []AttemptState{StateInDoubt},
    66			reasonCode: firstNonEmpty(reasonCode, ReasonResolvedAbsent),
    67			detail:     detail,
    68			setSettled: true,
    69		})
    70	}
    71	
    72	// ResolveUnresolved parks an IN_DOUBT attempt as UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT.
    73	//
    74	// This is the fail-closed outcome, not an error path: the symbol stays blocked
    75	// until an operator resolves it, which is the correct behaviour when a live
    76	// account's state cannot be established.
    77	func (a *Attempt) ResolveUnresolved(ctx context.Context, reasonCode, detail string) error {
    78		return a.transition(ctx, StateUnresolvedInDoubt, transitionOpts{
    79			from:       []AttemptState{StateInDoubt},
    80			reasonCode: firstNonEmpty(reasonCode, ReasonResolutionExhausted),
    81			detail:     detail,
    82			setSettled: true,
    83		})
    84	}
    85	
    86	// UnresolvedAttempts returns every attempt parked as UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT, oldest
    87	// first.
    88	//
    89	// PendingAttempts deliberately does not include them — UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT is a
    90	// terminal state — but they still block their symbol, and that block has to
    91	// survive a restart. This is how a caller finds them again.
    92	func (j *Journal) UnresolvedAttempts(ctx context.Context) ([]AttemptRecord, error) {
    93		rows, err := j.db.QueryContext(ctx, attemptSelect+
    94			` WHERE state = ? ORDER BY recorded_at, rowid`, string(StateUnresolvedInDoubt))
    95		if err != nil {
    96			return nil, fmt.Errorf("journal: listing unresolved attempts: %w", err)
    97		}
    98		defer rows.Close()
    99	
   100		var out []AttemptRecord
   101		for rows.Next() {
   102			rec, err := scanAttempt(rows)
   103			if err != nil {
   104				return nil, err
   105			}
   106			out = append(out, rec)
   107		}
   108		if err := rows.Err(); err != nil {
   109			return nil, fmt.Errorf("journal: listing unresolved attempts: %w", err)
   110		}
   111		return out, nil
   112	}
   113	
   114	// OperatorResolve is the only exit from UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT.
   115	//
   116	// It requires a terminal target (CONFIRMED or FAILED_CONFIRMED), the operator's
   117	// identity and a note, because this is a human overriding the machine's "I cannot
   118	// tell" about a live account — the audit trail is the point. Confirming also
   119	// requires the broker order id the operator found.
   120	func (j *Journal) OperatorResolve(ctx context.Context, attemptID string, to AttemptState, operator, brokerOrderID, note string) error {
   121		switch to {
   122		case StateConfirmed, StateFailedConfirmed:
   123		default:
   124			return fmt.Errorf("%w: an operator may only close an attempt as CONFIRMED or FAILED_CONFIRMED, not %s",
   125				ErrInvalidRequest, to)
   126		}
   127		if strings.TrimSpace(operator) == "" {
   128			return fmt.Errorf("%w: operator resolution requires the operator's identity", ErrInvalidRequest)
   129		}
   130		if strings.TrimSpace(note) == "" {
   131			return fmt.Errorf("%w: operator resolution requires a note explaining what was verified", ErrInvalidRequest)
   132		}
   133		if to == StateConfirmed && strings.TrimSpace(brokerOrderID) == "" {
   134			return fmt.Errorf("%w: confirming by hand requires the broker order id the operator found", ErrInvalidRequest)
   135		}
   100		return nil
   101	}
   102	
   103	// ResolveConfirmedWithLineage confirms an attempt and records the replace edge it
   104	// discovered, in one transaction.
   105	//
   106	// One transaction is the requirement, not an optimisation (order-execution: "두
   107	// 기록은 동일 트랜잭션에서 커밋된다"). A confirmed amend without its edge would
   108	// leave the engine holding an order id that no longer exists at the broker, with
   109	// nothing to point at the order that took its place.
   110	func (a *Attempt) ResolveConfirmedWithLineage(ctx context.Context, edge LineageEdge, reasonCode, detail string) error {
   111		if err := edge.validate(); err != nil {
   112			return err
   113		}
   114		if edge.Relation == "" {
   115			edge.Relation = RelationReplaces
   116		}
   117		if edge.Relation != RelationReplaces {
   118			return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown lineage relation %q", ErrInvalidRequest, edge.Relation)
   119		}
   120		return a.resolveWithLineage(ctx, edge, firstNonEmpty(reasonCode, ReasonResolvedFound), detail)
   121	}
   122	
   123	// resolveWithLineage performs the settle and the edge insert in one
   124	// BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction.
   125	func (a *Attempt) resolveWithLineage(ctx context.Context, edge LineageEdge, reasonCode, detail string) error {
   126		a.mu.Lock()
   127		defer a.mu.Unlock()
   128	
   129		from := []AttemptState{StateInDoubt, StateAcked}
   130		now := a.j.nowString()
   131	
   132		tx, err := a.j.db.BeginTx(ctx, nil)
   133		if err != nil {
   134			return fmt.Errorf("journal: starting the lineage transaction for %s: %w", a.id, err)
   135		}
   136		defer tx.Rollback()
   137	
   138		var current AttemptState
   139		if err := tx.QueryRowContext(ctx,
   140			"SELECT state FROM mutation_attempts WHERE id = ?", a.id).Scan(&current); err != nil {
   141			if errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows) {
   142				return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s", ErrAttemptNotFound, a.id)
   143			}
   144			return fmt.Errorf("journal: reading attempt %s: %w", a.id, err)
   145		}
   146		if err := checkTransitionAllowed(a.id, current, StateConfirmed, from); err != nil {
   147			return err
   148		}
   149	
   150		// The child's identifier is stored exactly as the broker sent it, the same
   151		// rule ResolveConfirmed follows (order-execution "브로커 식별자의 opaque
   152		// 취급": 저장 SHALL 원문, 비교 SHALL 바이트 동일). edge.validate above already
   153		// did the only job trimming has here — judging whether there is a name at
   154		// all. Storing a trimmed copy would put an identifier the broker never issued
   155		// in the attempt row, and the later byte comparison would silently miss it.
   156		res, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
   157			`UPDATE mutation_attempts
   158			    SET state = ?, settled_at = ?, broker_order_id = ?, reason_code = ?, detail = ?
   159			  WHERE id = ? AND state = ?`,
   160			string(StateConfirmed), now, edge.ChildOrderID, reasonCode, detail,
   161			a.id, string(current))
   162		if err != nil {
   163			return fmt.Errorf("journal: confirming attempt %s: %w", a.id, err)
   164		}
   165		affected, err := res.RowsAffected()
   166		if err != nil {
   167			return fmt.Errorf("journal: confirming attempt %s: %w", a.id, err)
   168		}
   169		if affected != 1 {
   170			return &StateError{AttemptID: a.id, Want: from, Got: current, To: StateConfirmed}
   171		}
   172	
   173		if _, err := tx.ExecContext(ctx,
   174			`INSERT INTO attempt_transitions (attempt_id, from_state, to_state, at, reason_code, detail)
   175			 VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)`,
   100	}
   101	
   102	// releaseThawedProposal 은 그 attempt 의 intent 를 무장한 발의를 해제 판정 함수로 풂. 실패는 결과에만 남김 — 해소는
   103	// 이미 커밋됐고, 다음 기동 따라잡기가 같은 판정으로 푼다(D−2.5).
   104	func (s *PositionPolicyCommandService) releaseThawedProposal(ctx context.Context, repo attemptThawRepository,
   105		rec journal.AttemptRecord, result *attemptthaw.Result) {
   106		intent, err := repo.LookupIntent(ctx, rec.IntentID)
   107		if err != nil {
   108			result.ReleaseError = err.Error()
   109			return
   110		}
   111		armed, err := repo.ArmedExitProposals(ctx, intent.AccountRef)
   112		if err != nil {
   113			result.ReleaseError = err.Error()
   114			return
   115		}
   116		for _, a := range armed {
   117			if a.IntentID != rec.IntentID {
   118				continue
   119			}
   120			result.PositionID = a.PositionID
   121			_, released, err := repo.ReleaseUnacceptedExitProposal(ctx, a.PositionID, a.IntentID, journal.ProposalRefused)
   122			if err != nil {
   123				result.ReleaseError = err.Error()
   124				s.recordThawReleaseFailure(ctx, rec, a.PositionID, err)
   125				return
   126			}
   127			result.ProposalReleased = released
   128			return
   129		}
   130	}
   131	
   132	// recordThawReleaseFailure 는 해소는 커밋됐는데 발의 해제가 실패한 사실을 critical 로 남김 — 관측 루프가 다음 판정 진입에서
   133	// 같은 판정으로 풀지만(noteHeldProposal), 운영자가 결과를 기다리는 명령 밖에서도 보이게.
   134	func (s *PositionPolicyCommandService) recordThawReleaseFailure(ctx context.Context, rec journal.AttemptRecord,
   135		positionID string, cause error) {
   136		if s.notices == nil {
   137			return
   138		}
   139		_ = s.notices.RecordCritical(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), obs.Event{
   140			Type:  obs.EventExitLiquidationDelayed,
   141			Key:   string(obs.EventExitLiquidationDelayed) + "|" + positionID + "|thaw-release:" + rec.ID,
   142			Title: "해동한 attempt 의 발의 해제가 실패했다",
   143			Body: fmt.Sprintf("운영자가 attempt %s 를 비수용으로 닫았으나 포지션 %s 의 발의 해제 쓰기가 실패했다. 다음 관측이 같은 판정으로 "+
   144				"다시 풀고, 안 되면 다음 기동이 푼다. 원인: %v", rec.ID, positionID, cause),
   145			Fields: map[string]any{obs.FieldAttemptID: rec.ID, "position_id": positionID},
   146		}, 0)
   147	}
   148	
   149	func thawTarget(req attemptthaw.Request) (journal.AttemptState, error) {
   150		for _, f := range []struct{ name, value string }{
   151			{"attempt", req.AttemptID}, {"operator", req.Operator}, {"approval", req.Approval}, {"note", req.Note},
   152		} {
   153			if strings.TrimSpace(f.value) == "" {
   154				return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: the %s is required", attemptthaw.ErrInvalidRequest, f.name)
   155			}
   156		}
   157		switch strings.ToUpper(strings.TrimSpace(req.Target)) {
   158		case attemptthaw.TargetFailedConfirmed:
   159			return journal.StateFailedConfirmed, nil
   160		case attemptthaw.TargetConfirmed:
   161			if strings.TrimSpace(req.BrokerOrderID) == "" {
   162				return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: confirming needs the broker order number the operator found", attemptthaw.ErrInvalidRequest)
   163			}
   164			return journal.StateConfirmed, nil
   165		default:
   166			return "", fmt.Errorf("%w: the target must be %s or %s", attemptthaw.ErrInvalidRequest,
   167				attemptthaw.TargetFailedConfirmed, attemptthaw.TargetConfirmed)
   168		}
   169	}
    28	func (c *Client) Accounts(ctx context.Context) ([]domain.Account, error) {
    29		c.mu.Lock()
    30		defer c.mu.Unlock()
    31		return c.accountsLocked(ctx)
    32	}
    33	
    34	// accountsLocked fetches and adapts the account list while the caller holds
    35	// c.mu. Both public discovery and lazy account scoping use this one path so a
    36	// public Accounts request that is already in flight can prime the following
    37	// account-scoped request without a second HTTP call.
    38	func (c *Client) accountsLocked(ctx context.Context) ([]domain.Account, error) {
    39		var raw []apiAccount
    40		if err := c.get(ctx, "/api/v1/accounts", nil, &raw); err != nil {
    41			return nil, err
    42		}
    43		selected := c.accountSeq.Load()
    44		if selected == 0 && len(raw) > 0 && raw[0].AccountSeq > 0 {
    45			c.accountSeq.Store(int64(raw[0].AccountSeq))
    46		} else if selected > 0 && !c.accountSeqExplicit {
    47			if len(raw) == 0 || raw[0].AccountSeq <= 0 || int64(raw[0].AccountSeq) != selected {
    48				return nil, fmt.Errorf(
    49					"account sequence drift: selected implicit sequence %d no longer matches the first account record",
    50					selected,
    51				)
    52			}
    53		}
    54		// a131: 계좌 번호가 프로세스에 들어오는 유일한 입구 — 받은 번호를 로그 치환 규칙에 등록(메모리 쓰기 하나, 반환·오류 불변).
    55		registerAccountNumbers(raw)
    56		return adaptAccounts(raw), nil
    57	}
    58	
    59	// SelectedAccountSeq reports the account sequence the next account-scoped
    60	// request would use. A false result means the value is unresolved or invalid.
    61	// It performs no discovery and is used by engine startup to prove its journal
    62	// account number and official request header name the same first record.
    63	func (c *Client) SelectedAccountSeq() (int, bool) {
    64		seq := c.accountSeq.Load()
    65		return int(seq), seq > 0
    66	}
    67	
    68	// adaptAccounts converts a slice of official API account records to the
    69	// canonical domain representation. It is a pure function (no I/O).
    70	//
    71	// Mapping rationale (cross-referenced with internal/client/account.go WTS adapter):
    72	//
    73	//   - accountSeq (int) → ID (string):
    74	//     WTS uses item.Key as the stable account identifier passed to subsequent
    75	//     calls. The official equivalent is accountSeq (an integer key). We
    76	//     convert int→string to match the domain.Account.ID type.
    77	//
    78	//   - accountNo (string) → DisplayName:
    79	//     WTS puts item.DisplayName in domain.Account.DisplayName. The official
    80	//     accountNo is the human-readable account number and is the closest
    81	//     analogue to the displayed identity.
    82	//
    83	//   - accountType (string enum) → Type:
    84	//     WTS copies item.Type verbatim. We do the same with the official enum
    85	//     value (e.g. "BROKERAGE") to avoid an unnecessary translation layer.
    86	//
    87	//   - Name, Markets, Currency, Primary: not present in the official accounts
    88	//     endpoint; left as zero values. Currency and Primary can be enriched by
    89	//     callers if needed.
    90	func adaptAccounts(raw []apiAccount) []domain.Account {
    91		out := make([]domain.Account, 0, len(raw))
    92		for _, a := range raw {
    93			out = append(out, domain.Account{
    94				ID:          strconv.Itoa(a.AccountSeq), // official accountSeq → stable call-time key
    95				DisplayName: a.AccountNo,                // official accountNo  → human display value
    96				Type:        a.AccountType,              // official accountType → raw enum (e.g. BROKERAGE)
    97				// Name     — not available from official API
    98				// Markets  — not available from official API
    99				// Currency — not available from official API
   100				// Primary  — not available from official API (no primaryKey concept in this endpoint)

    75	//
    76	//   - RECORDED only: nothing was ever sent, so the attempt is closed as
    77	//     NOT_DISPATCHED and blocks nothing. This is the whole payoff of writing the
    78	//     intent before dispatching — the ambiguous window is bounded by one fsync.
    79	//   - DISPATCH_STARTED: the request may have reached the broker, so the attempt
    80	//     becomes IN_DOUBT and its symbol is blocked until resolution finishes.
    81	//   - ACKED and IN_DOUBT: left as they are, still blocking. An ACK that was never
    82	//     confirmed and an unresolved doubt both need the resolution pass, not a
    83	//     state change from the recovery pass.
    84	//
    85	// It is idempotent: running it again neither re-transitions nor loses the blocks.
    86	func (j *Journal) RecoverPending(ctx context.Context) (RecoveryReport, error) {
    87		pending, err := j.PendingAttempts(ctx)
    88		if err != nil {
    89			return RecoveryReport{}, err
    90		}
    91	
    92		report := RecoveryReport{}
    93		for _, rec := range pending {
    94			switch rec.State {
    95			case StateRecorded:
    96				handle := j.handleFor(rec)
    97				if err := handle.Settle(ctx, StateNotDispatched, ReasonRestartNotDispatched,
    98					"found at startup with no dispatch recorded"); err != nil {
    99					return report, fmt.Errorf("journal: closing attempt %s at startup: %w", rec.ID, err)
   100				}
   101				report.NotDispatched = append(report.NotDispatched, rec.ID)
   102	
   103			case StateDispatchStarted:
   104				handle := j.handleFor(rec)
   105				if err := handle.MarkInDoubt(ctx, ReasonRestartInDoubt,
   106					"process stopped after dispatch started; outcome unknown"); err != nil {
   107					return report, fmt.Errorf("journal: marking attempt %s in doubt at startup: %w", rec.ID, err)
   108				}
   109				report.InDoubt = append(report.InDoubt, rec.ID)
   110				blocked, err := j.blockedFor(ctx, rec, StateInDoubt)
   111				if err != nil {
   112					return report, err
   113				}
   114				report.Blocked = append(report.Blocked, blocked)
   115	
   116			case StateAcked, StateInDoubt:
   117				blocked, err := j.blockedFor(ctx, rec, rec.State)
   118				if err != nil {
   119					return report, err
   120				}
   121				report.Blocked = append(report.Blocked, blocked)
   122			}
   123		}
   124		return report, nil
   125	}

   500	// MarkAcked records the broker's acknowledgement and the order id it named.
   501	func (a *Attempt) MarkAcked(ctx context.Context, brokerOrderID string) error {
   502		return a.transition(ctx, StateAcked, transitionOpts{
   503			from:          []AttemptState{StateDispatchStarted},
   504			brokerOrderID: brokerOrderID,
   505		})
   506	}
   507	
   508	// MarkInDoubt records that the outcome is unknown. It is not terminal: the
   509	// resolution procedure has to finish before the attempt can be settled.
   510	func (a *Attempt) MarkInDoubt(ctx context.Context, reasonCode, detail string) error {
   511		return a.transition(ctx, StateInDoubt, transitionOpts{
   512			from:       []AttemptState{StateDispatchStarted, StateAcked},
   513			reasonCode: reasonCode,
   514			detail:     detail,
   515		})
   516	}
   517	
   518	// Settle closes the attempt in a terminal state.
   519	func (a *Attempt) Settle(ctx context.Context, state AttemptState, reasonCode, detail string) error {
   520		if !state.IsTerminal() {
   521			return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s is not a terminal state", ErrInvalidRequest, state)
   522		}
   523		return a.transition(ctx, state, transitionOpts{
   524			from:       []AttemptState{StateRecorded, StateDispatchStarted, StateAcked, StateInDoubt},
   525			reasonCode: reasonCode,
   526			detail:     detail,
   527			setSettled: true,
   528		})
   529	}
   530

   532		// from is the set of states the transition is legal from. Empty means "any

    48	func (r *Recovery) confirmAcked(ctx context.Context, rec journal.AttemptRecord) bool {
    49		intent, err := r.opts.Journal.LookupIntent(ctx, rec.IntentID)
    50		if err != nil {
    51			r.alertAcked(ctx, rec, "", fmt.Sprintf("the intent could not be read: %v", err))
    52			return false
    53		}
    54		// a198 D9: 취소의 ACKED 는 브로커가 접수를 답했고(번호 기록) 최종 쓰기만 남은 상태임 — 취소에는 접수 뒤 확인 읽기가 없음.
    55		// 디스패치가 하려던 쓰기(같은 상태·같은 번호)를 마칠 뿐 읽기·추론이 없음. 그 번호는 취소가 새로 받은 식별자라 대상 판정에 안 쓰임.
    56		if rec.Kind == journal.KindCancel && strings.TrimSpace(rec.BrokerOrderID) != "" {
    57			attempt, err := r.opts.Journal.Resume(ctx, rec.ID)
    58			if err == nil {
    59				err = attempt.ResolveConfirmed(ctx, rec.BrokerOrderID, ReasonBootAckedCancelCompleted,
    60					"restart recovery completed the acknowledged cancel's final write with its recorded order number")
    61			}
    62			if err != nil {
    63				r.alertAcked(ctx, rec, intent.Symbol, fmt.Sprintf("completing the acknowledged cancel: the ledger write failed: %v", err))
    64				return false
    65			}
    66			return true
    67		}
    68		if rec.Kind != journal.KindPlace {
    69			r.alertAcked(ctx, rec, intent.Symbol, "a CANCEL/AMEND acknowledgement has no read-back rule; it stays ACKED")
    70			return false
    71		}
    72		if strings.TrimSpace(rec.BrokerOrderID) == "" {
    73			r.alertAcked(ctx, rec, intent.Symbol, "the acknowledgement recorded no broker order number to read back")
    74			return false
    75		}
    76		if err := execgw.ConfirmPlacedOrder(ctx, r.opts.Resolver.Order, rec.BrokerOrderID, intent.Symbol); err != nil {
    77			r.alertAcked(ctx, rec, intent.Symbol, "the read-back did not confirm it: "+withoutBrokerBody(err))
    78			return false
    79		}
    80		attempt, err := r.opts.Journal.Resume(ctx, rec.ID)
    81		if err == nil {
    82			err = attempt.ResolveConfirmed(ctx, rec.BrokerOrderID, ReasonBootAckedConfirmed,
    83				"restart recovery read the recorded order number back byte-for-byte on the same symbol")
    84		}
    85		if err != nil {
    86			r.alertAcked(ctx, rec, intent.Symbol, fmt.Sprintf("the read-back matched but the ledger write failed: %v", err))
    87			return false
    88		}
    89		return true
    90	}

   170		}

   172		// statement onto one connection is what makes "BEGIN IMMEDIATE then commit
   173		// then submit" hold without SQLITE_BUSY races between pooled connections.
   174		db.SetMaxOpenConns(1)

   181		}

   184		// Corruption is checked before the schema is touched: a migration against a
   185		// damaged file would write on top of the damage. A corrupt journal means we
   186		// cannot say what the engine did last, so startup is refused rather than
   187		// continued from a record we know is wrong.
   188		if err := j.checkIntegrity(ctx); err != nil {
   189			db.Close()
   190			return nil, err
   191		}
   192		plan := defaultMigrationPlan()
   193		if opts.migrationOverride != nil {
   194			plan = *opts.migrationOverride
   195		}
   196		if err := j.migrate(ctx, plan); err != nil {
   197			db.Close()
   198			return nil, err
   199		}

    28	func (c *Client) Accounts(ctx context.Context) ([]domain.Account, error) {
    29		c.mu.Lock()
    30		defer c.mu.Unlock()
    31		return c.accountsLocked(ctx)
    32	}
    33	
    34	// accountsLocked fetches and adapts the account list while the caller holds
    35	// c.mu. Both public discovery and lazy account scoping use this one path so a
    36	// public Accounts request that is already in flight can prime the following
    37	// account-scoped request without a second HTTP call.
    38	func (c *Client) accountsLocked(ctx context.Context) ([]domain.Account, error) {
    39		var raw []apiAccount
    40		if err := c.get(ctx, "/api/v1/accounts", nil, &raw); err != nil {
    41			return nil, err
    42		}
    43		selected := c.accountSeq.Load()
    44		if selected == 0 && len(raw) > 0 && raw[0].AccountSeq > 0 {

   678	func checkTransitionAllowed(attemptID string, from, to AttemptState, allowed []AttemptState) error {

   681		}

     1	package journal
     2	
     3	import (
     4		"errors"
     5		"fmt"
     6	)
     7	
     8	// ErrIllegalTransition means the requested state change is not in the lifecycle
     9	// table. It is not a retryable condition: it means the caller's model of the
    10	// attempt and the journal's record disagree.
    11	var ErrIllegalTransition = errors.New("journal: illegal attempt state transition")
    12	
    13	// stateNone is the pseudo-state an attempt has before it is written.
    14	const stateNone AttemptState = ""
    15	
    16	// transitionTable is the MutationAttempt lifecycle from the order-execution spec.
    17	//
    18	//	(new) ──► RECORDED ──► DISPATCH_STARTED ──► ACKED ──► CONFIRMED
    19	//	              │               │               │
    20	//	              │               ├──► IN_DOUBT ◄─┘
    21	//	              │               ├──► NOT_DISPATCHED
    22	//	              ▼               └──► FAILED_CONFIRMED
    23	//	        NOT_DISPATCHED
    24	//
    25	// The edges that are deliberately absent are the interesting part:
    26	//
    27	//   - RECORDED cannot reach CONFIRMED, IN_DOUBT or FAILED_CONFIRMED. Nothing was
    28	//     sent, so there is nothing to confirm, doubt or have rejected. The only close
    29	//     is NOT_DISPATCHED, which is also what a restart applies.
    30	//   - DISPATCH_STARTED cannot reach CONFIRMED directly. Confirmation goes through
    31	//     an ACK (or through IN_DOUBT resolution), so "confirmed" always has a
    32	//     traceable source.
    33	//   - IN_DOUBT cannot reach NOT_DISPATCHED. Once the request may have left, "it
    34	//     never happened" is not something we can assert; proving absence lands on
    35	//     FAILED_CONFIRMED, and failing to prove anything lands on
    36	//     UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT.
    37	//   - CONFIRMED, NOT_DISPATCHED and FAILED_CONFIRMED have no outgoing edges at
    38	//     all. UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT keeps two, reachable only through an explicit
    39	//     operator resolution API (task 2.7); no automatic path uses them, and Settle
    40	//     cannot because it refuses to act on a terminal attempt.
    41	var transitionTable = map[AttemptState][]AttemptState{
    42		stateNone:              {StateRecorded},
    43		StateRecorded:          {StateDispatchStarted, StateNotDispatched},
    44		StateDispatchStarted:   {StateAcked, StateInDoubt, StateNotDispatched, StateFailedConfirmed},
    45		StateAcked:             {StateConfirmed, StateInDoubt},
    46		StateInDoubt:           {StateConfirmed, StateFailedConfirmed, StateUnresolvedInDoubt},
    47		StateUnresolvedInDoubt: {StateConfirmed, StateFailedConfirmed},
    48		StateConfirmed:         nil,
    49		StateNotDispatched:     nil,
    50		StateFailedConfirmed:   nil,
    51	}
    52	
    53	// ValidateTransition reports whether from → to is in the lifecycle table.
    54	func ValidateTransition(from, to AttemptState) error {
    55		allowed, known := transitionTable[from]
    56		if !known {
    57			return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown state %q", ErrIllegalTransition, from)
    58		}
    59		if _, knownTo := transitionTable[to]; !knownTo || to == stateNone {
    60			return fmt.Errorf("%w: unknown target state %q", ErrIllegalTransition, to)
    61		}
    62		for _, candidate := range allowed {
    63			if candidate == to {
    64				return nil
    65			}
    66		}
    67		return fmt.Errorf("%w: %s cannot move to %s", ErrIllegalTransition, from, to)
    68	}
    69	
    70	// AllowedNext returns the states an attempt may move to next. Terminal states
    71	// return nil, except UNRESOLVED_IN_DOUBT (see transitionTable).
    72	func AllowedNext(from AttemptState) []AttemptState {
    73		allowed := transitionTable[from]
    74		if len(allowed) == 0 {
    75			return nil
    76		}
    77		out := make([]AttemptState, len(allowed))
    78		copy(out, allowed)
    79		return out
    80	}
    81	
    82	// Reason codes the lifecycle itself writes. They are stable strings: operators and
    83	// the alerting path read them.
    84	const (
    85		// ReasonRestartNotDispatched closes an attempt that never got past RECORDED.
    86		ReasonRestartNotDispatched = "restart_not_dispatched"
    87		// ReasonRestartInDoubt marks an attempt that was mid-dispatch when the
    88		// process died.
    89		ReasonRestartInDoubt = "restart_in_doubt"
    90	
    91		// ReasonDispatchNotSent is a provably unsent mutation.
    92		ReasonDispatchNotSent = "dispatch_not_sent"
    93		// ReasonDispatchRejected is a well-formed broker refusal.
    94		ReasonDispatchRejected = "dispatch_rejected_by_broker"
    95		// ReasonDispatchAmbiguous is the "we cannot tell" outcome.
    96		ReasonDispatchAmbiguous = "dispatch_outcome_unknown"
    97		// ReasonBrokerAcknowledged is a broker ack that named an order.
    98		ReasonBrokerAcknowledged = "broker_acknowledged"
    99		// ReasonAckWithoutOrderID is an ack we cannot track: no order number came
   100		// back, so the mutation may exist and be unfindable by id.
   101		ReasonAckWithoutOrderID = "ack_without_order_id"
   102		// ReasonUnknownDispatchClass is a dispatch function returning something this
   103		// build does not classify.
   104		ReasonUnknownDispatchClass = "unknown_dispatch_class"
   105	)

Current binding checks prevent new mismatches (`internal/app/engine/interlock.go:741–758`), but the new query trusts old labels (`internal/journal/a199_order_path_evidence.go:32`).
   **Repro:** Old broker list `[A(number empty), B(number B)]` → confirmed engine order actually on A, journal labelled B → upgrade, select B, stale B attestation → exit-only admitted without B’s order-path evidence. Historical occurrence not established.

2. **P0 — Recoverable broker acceptance cannot establish proof before startup refuses.** ACK and CONFIRMED are separate writes (`internal/journal/dispatch.go:181,199`). Boot can confirm ACKED PLACE by broker readback (`internal/reconcile/acked_boot.go:76–82`), but interlock rejection occurs first (`internal/app/engine/engine.go:581–592`; recovery assembled later at `cmd/tossctl/engine.go:692`).
   **Repro:** No earlier CONFIRMED PLACE; broker accepts first engine PLACE; crash after ACK persistence; attestation expires before restart → proof query rejects ACKED → startup refuses before recovery, leaving remaining holdings without stop-loss observation. This follows the specified CONFIRMED-only contract but exposes a recovery/design gap.

3. **P2 — Default source is not authenticated engine provenance; no current production bypass found.** `execgw.New` converts empty/whitespace source to `"engine"` (`internal/execgw/gateway.go:168–187`). Production constructors explicitly supply `"engine"` (`internal/app/engine/gateway.go:327`) and `"flatten-all"` (`cmd/tossctl/flatten.go:249`).
   **Repro:** A helper using the real journal, empty Source, and successful PLACE creates qualifying evidence; no such production helper was located. Treat this as a latent provenance weakness, not a demonstrated runtime exploit.

4. **P2 — Current writer/account checks: no additional cross-account match found.** Sole production SQL intent insertion is `internal/journal/durability.go:397–404`, preserving supplied account/source; sole production intent builder is `internal/execgw/gateway.go:1045–1056`. Engine supplies trimmed official `accountNo`, not a mask or sequence (`internal/official/reads.go:95`; `internal/app/engine/interlock.go:745`). Flatten uses the first nonempty account number (`cmd/tossctl/flatten.go:220–225`) but its source is excluded.
   **Repro/check:** Store account A’s confirmed PLACE, query B → false. Multiple accounts can share the default journal: its path is installation-scoped, not account-scoped (`internal/app/engine/engine.go:678–685`).

5. **P2 — Manual orders and verification do not seed this evidence.** Manual placement calls Trading directly (`cmd/tossctl/order.go:229`); verification calls its broker (`internal/verifylive/mutate.go:228`) and explicitly uses a separate record (`internal/verifylive/record.go:300`). Production-journal backfill writes trade outcomes (`tools/a130-outcome-backfill/main.go:214–219`).
   **Repro/check:** Only manual/verify/flatten orders → no qualifying engine row. Test-opening sites were searched, but every fixture’s isolation was not exhaustively traced; no production-journal test contamination is established.

6. **P2 — CONFIRMED normally includes broker acceptance and an order ID; AMEND alone cannot qualify.** Dispatch without an ID becomes IN_DOUBT (`internal/journal/dispatch.go:170–178`); resolution also requires an ID (`internal/journal/resolution.go:48–54`). Operator confirmation is another route, requiring identity, note and ID, so CONFIRMED is not exclusively automatic broker evidence (`internal/journal/resolution.go:120–134`). Query filters **attempt kind PLACE** (`internal/journal/a199_order_path_evidence.go:33`).
   **Repro/check:** Confirmed AMEND-only intent → false; operator-confirmed engine PLACE → true.

1. **P0 — Legacy rows can attest the wrong account (conditional historical input).** Before `93165f969`, `internal/app/engine/interlock.go:243` selected the first **nonempty account number**, while `internal/official/client.go:156` selected the **first account sequence** for broker calls. Current binding checks prevent new mismatches (`internal/app/engine/interlock.go:741–758`), but the new query trusts old labels (`internal/journal/a199_order_path_evidence.go:32`).
   **Repro:** Old broker list `[A(number empty), B(number B)]` → confirmed engine order actually on A, journal labelled B → upgrade, select B, stale B attestation → exit-only admitted without B’s order-path evidence. Historical occurrence not established.

2. **P0 — Recoverable broker acceptance cannot establish proof before startup refuses.** ACK and CONFIRMED are separate writes (`internal/journal/dispatch.go:181,199`). Boot can confirm ACKED PLACE by broker readback (`internal/reconcile/acked_boot.go:76–82`), but interlock rejection occurs first (`internal/app/engine/engine.go:581–592`; recovery assembled later at `cmd/tossctl/engine.go:692`).
   **Repro:** No earlier CONFIRMED PLACE; broker accepts first engine PLACE; crash after ACK persistence; attestation expires before restart → proof query rejects ACKED → startup refuses before recovery, leaving remaining holdings without stop-loss observation. This follows the specified CONFIRMED-only contract but exposes a recovery/design gap.

3. **P2 — Default source is not authenticated engine provenance; no current production bypass found.** `execgw.New` converts empty/whitespace source to `"engine"` (`internal/execgw/gateway.go:168–187`). Production constructors explicitly supply `"engine"` (`internal/app/engine/gateway.go:327`) and `"flatten-all"` (`cmd/tossctl/flatten.go:249`).
   **Repro:** A helper using the real journal, empty Source, and successful PLACE creates qualifying evidence; no such production helper was located. Treat this as a latent provenance weakness, not a demonstrated runtime exploit.

4. **P2 — Current writer/account checks: no additional cross-account match found.** Sole production SQL intent insertion is `internal/journal/durability.go:397–404`, preserving supplied account/source; sole production intent builder is `internal/execgw/gateway.go:1045–1056`. Engine supplies trimmed official `accountNo`, not a mask or sequence (`internal/official/reads.go:95`; `internal/app/engine/interlock.go:745`). Flatten uses the first nonempty account number (`cmd/tossctl/flatten.go:220–225`) but its source is excluded.
   **Repro/check:** Store account A’s confirmed PLACE, query B → false. Multiple accounts can share the default journal: its path is installation-scoped, not account-scoped (`internal/app/engine/engine.go:678–685`).

5. **P2 — Manual orders and verification do not seed this evidence.** Manual placement calls Trading directly (`cmd/tossctl/order.go:229`); verification calls its broker (`internal/verifylive/mutate.go:228`) and explicitly uses a separate record (`internal/verifylive/record.go:300`). Production-journal backfill writes trade outcomes (`tools/a130-outcome-backfill/main.go:214–219`).
   **Repro/check:** Only manual/verify/flatten orders → no qualifying engine row. Test-opening sites were searched, but every fixture’s isolation was not exhaustively traced; no production-journal test contamination is established.

6. **P2 — CONFIRMED normally includes broker acceptance and an order ID; AMEND alone cannot qualify.** Dispatch without an ID becomes IN_DOUBT (`internal/journal/dispatch.go:170–178`); resolution also requires an ID (`internal/journal/resolution.go:48–54`). Operator confirmation is another route, requiring identity, note and ID, so CONFIRMED is not exclusively automatic broker evidence (`internal/journal/resolution.go:120–134`). Query filters **attempt kind PLACE** (`internal/journal/a199_order_path_evidence.go:33`).
   **Repro/check:** Confirmed AMEND-only intent → false; operator-confirmed engine PLACE → true.

7. **P2 — No demonstrated legacy masking/source migration or retention loss.** Initial schema commit `b4c38340f`, `internal/journal/schema.go:57,65`, already required account/source; initial engine gateway commit `a1efa5ab3`, `internal/app/engine/gateway.go:55,123`, used `"engine"`. No production intent deletion/rewrite or attempt retention deletion was located. Boot sweeps reservations/nonces (`internal/app/engine/engine.go:696,715`); backup uses copying `VACUUM INTO` (`internal/journal/backup.go:76`).
   **Repro/check:** Retained ordinary CONFIRMED row survives these paths. Renumbered/reformatted accounts or replaced journals would fail matching, but no normal-install migration producing that condition was established.

8. **P2 — Read failures fail closed; no classifier-before-open path found.** Journal opens at `internal/app/engine/engine.go:507`, proof runs at `:587`. SQLite uses one connection (`internal/journal/journal.go:174`), WAL/busy timeout (`:221–224`; default five seconds at `:36`); proof captures constructor context (`internal/app/engine/exit_only_start.go:83`).
   **Repro:** Cancel that context before proof lookup → startup refusal despite existing evidence. Normal boot contention frequency cannot be inferred from source; single connection alone does not establish a deadlock.

9. **P2 — No circular exit-only bootstrap found.** The only production assignment to `AutomationStatus.ExitOnly=true` is `internal/app/engine/interlock.go:608`, after successful proof classification (`:564`; `exit_only_start.go:139–146`). Missing entry gate clears it (`exit_only_start.go:163`). Watch copies the flag and independently reads proof; it never sets automation status (`attestation_watch.go:271–272`).
   **Repro/check:** Empty journal + stale attestation → refusal, therefore no self-generated first exit evidence. Valid attestation → verified start → confirmed exit → later stale restart is the legitimate noncircular sequence.

10. **Review limits:** Static source/history inspection only; no tests, trading CLI, configuration access, or file changes. ai-memory lookup failed under network restrictions; graph/AST tooling was not run.
